# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENTS LOG SCRIPTED LOCALISATION
# ============================================================================
# Dedicated scripted localisation for events-log controls and history rows.
# Uses the filtered/sorted view arrays populated by rebuild_events_log_history_view.
# ============================================================================

defined_text = {
	name = GetEventsLogHistoryTypeView
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.major
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		localization_key = settings.major.colored
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.repeatable
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		localization_key = settings.minor_repeatable.colored
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.fire_once
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		localization_key = settings.minor_fire_once.colored
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.fallout_country_memory
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		localization_key = fallout.event_log.type
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = "Unknown"
	}
}

defined_text = {
	name = GetEventsLogFilterType
	text = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { settings_events_log_filter_type = 0 } }

	}
	text = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { settings_events_log_filter_type = 1 } }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.filter.major

	text = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { settings_events_log_filter_type = 2 } }

		trigger = { always = yes }

	}
}

defined_text = {
	name = GetEventsLogEventUniqueLabel
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_events_view_unique_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = 0
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.events.unique.yes
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.events.unique.no
	}

defined_text = {
	name = GetEventsLogAllEventsCheckboxSprite
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = events_log_clusters_tab
			check_variable = { global.disabled_event_clusters^num < 1 }
		}
		localization_key = "GFX_chaosx_checkbox_checked"
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = events_log_clusters_tab
		}
		localization_key = "GFX_chaosx_checkbox_unchecked"
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = { global.disabled_events^num < 1 }

		localization_key = "GFX_chaosx_checkbox_checked"
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = "GFX_chaosx_checkbox_unchecked"
	}
}

defined_text = {
	name = GetEventsLogHistoryRowText
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.fallout_country_memory
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_id_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:fallout_nzl_event_log.external_history_id
				compare = equals
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_has_secondary_actor_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:fallout_nzl_schema.zero
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		localization_key = fallout_nzl.event_log.row.external_partner
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_event_type_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = constant:event_system_event_type.fallout_country_memory
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		localization_key = fallout_nzl.event_log.row
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { random_faction_history_row_has_bound_alignment_result = yes }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.window.history.dynamic_entry.random_faction
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { random_faction_history_row_has_lost_alignment_result = yes }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.window.history.dynamic_entry.random_faction.lost
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { random_faction_history_row_has_recorded_alignment_context = yes }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.window.history.dynamic_entry.random_faction.unresolved
	}
	text = {
		trigger = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.events_log_view_cluster_id_entries^events_log_history_index
				value = 0
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.window.history.dynamic_entry.cluster
	}

	text = {
		trigger = { always = yes }
		localization_key = chaosx.events_log.window.history.dynamic_entry.event
	}
}
